General Information (Go to Renown)
Name		Source	Type	Tier	Description				Notes / Rage Advice
Renown		DMG2014/2024	Renown	Varies	Gaining renown with a particular group, such as a faction, organization, or community. See book for description.				- Except for starting Renown at character creation, a character starts with a Renown score of 0 with respect to a group. Completing an adventure that advances a group's interest increases a character's Renown score by 1 as determined by a DM. Completing an adventure as part of a mission directly assigned by that group increases a character's Renown score by 2 as determined by a DM. Highly significant quests can increase Renown score by 3 or 4 as determined by a DM. - A character with a Renown Score of 3+ with a group is typically considered a respected member of it, as either an official or honorary member. Some groups come with member benefits for being a member of that group. - A character can also lose Renown Score as written. A character that no longer meets the Renown Score requirement for a Renown Perk can't benefit from it until they reach the appropriate Renown Score once more. - A character can obtain Renown with and benefit from associated Renown Perks for any number of groups. However, a character can't become a respected member of a faction or organization (Renown Score 3+) if they are already a respected member of a faction or organization that is Hostile to it. The character can't become a respected member or have a Renown Score of 3+ with such a faction or organization without first reducing their Renown Score with the Hostile faction or organization to 0 and forfeit all associated Renown Perks. This voluntary reduction of Renown Score should be logged in either the downtime log or session log in which it occurs. (Note: A DM may allow a PC to infiltrate a Hostile faction or organization during an adventure and potentially benefit from Renown Perks with that faction that last for the duration of the adventure only) - Some groups have ranks and titles associated with achieving certain Renown Score thresholds with that group. Besides an indication of a member's recognition or title within the group, ranks have no mechanical effects. - A character can only benefit from Renown Perks from a community while physically present at that community's location.
Renown Perk		DMG2014/2024	Renown Perk	Varies	Gaining a perk as part of achieving a certain Renown Score within a group. See book for description.				- Renown Perks don't count against a game's item slots or gold allotment but cannot be sold or traded. - Characters that achieve a particular Renown Score within a group are eligible to receive Marks of Prestige, Story Outcomes, and trinkets that don't count against a session's item slots or gold allotment as determined by the DM.  - A DM can award other Renown Perks as part of an adventure without request provided their effects don't extend beyond the scope of the adventure. Both the 2014 and 2024 Dungeon Master's Guide provide suggestions of possible Renown Perks. Depending on the nature of the group, some of the suggested perks for Group Patrons described in Tasha's Cauldron of Everything are also appropriate as Renown Perks. - A DM can award other Renown Perks that are more mechanically significant and that persist beyond an adventure as part of an approved Rules request. A list of available Renown Perks for different groups can also be seen below that are available to DMs to award without a request.
Cult of the Dragon (Go to Renown)
Name		Source	Type	Tier	Description				Notes / Rage Advice
Cult of the Dragon		FRHOF / FRAIF	Group (Faction)	—	See Forgotten Realms: Heroes of Faerûn for a description, list of Renown rewards, and member benefits. See other sources for additional information.  Ranks - Dragonclaw: Renown Score 10+ - Dragonwing: Renown Score 15+ - Dragonfang: Renown Score 25+ - Dragonsoul: Renown Score 40+ - Wearer of Purple: Renown Score 50+				- See sources for description of member traits. - The Cult of the Dragon is one of the major factions of Faerûn. - The Cult of the Dragon are Hostile to the Emerald Enclave, the Harpers, the Lords' Alliance, the Order of the Gauntlet, and the Purple Dragon Knights. - For this faction's Member Benefits, you can either roll for or choose a Cult of the Dragon Trinket. Additionally, whenever you expend DTP while a respected member of this faction (Renown Score 3+), you must expend 3 GP per DTP expended to cover your tithing. - For the Renown Score 3+ benefit, you don't need to expend 1 GP per DTP expended while outside ----- if you are at a location with an accessible Cult of the Dragon hideout. - For the Renown Score 10 benefit, the magic item obtained is a T1 magic item of your choice in Allowed Content (Loot) and must be logged in the session or downtime log in which you reached this Renown score. - The Renown Score 25+ benefit can only be used during adventures or as overseen by a DM. - For the Renown Score 30 benefit, the magic item obtained is a T2 magic item of your choice in Allowed Content (Loot) and must be logged in the session or downtime log in which you reached this Renown score. - The Renown Score 40+ benefit can only be used during adventures or as overseen by a DM. - The Renown Score 50+ benefit can be reused the next time your Renown score increases by 5 or more since you last used it.
Harpers (Go to Renown)
Name		Source	Type	Tier	Description				Notes / Rage Advice
Harpers		FRHOF / FRAIF / DMG2014 / 2014 Basic Rules / Adventurers League	Group (Faction)	—	See Forgotten Realms: Heroes of Faerûn for a description, list of Renown rewards, and member benefits. See other sources for additional information.  The Harpers is a scattered network of spellcasters and spies who advocate equality and covertly oppose the abuse of power. The organization is benevolent, knowledgeable, and secretive. Bards and wizards of good alignments are commonly drawn to the Harpers.  Motto: Down with tyranny. Fairness and equality for all.  Goals - Gather information throughout Faerûn. - Promote fairness and equality by covert means. - Thwart tyrants and leaders, governments, and organizations that grow too powerful. - Aid the weak, poor and oppressed.  Beliefs - One can never have too much information or arcane knowledge. - Too much power leads to corruption. The abuse of magic must be closely monitored. - No one should be powerless.  Ranks - Watcher: Renown Score 3+ - Harpshadow: Renown Score 15+ - Brightcandle: Renown Score 25+ - Wise Owl: Renown Score 40+ - High Harper: Renown Score 50+				- The Harpers are one of the major factions of Faerûn. - The Harpers are Hostile to the Cult of the Dragon, the Red Wizards, the Zhentarim, and the Legion of Dread. - Traits: Harper agents are trained to act alone and rely on their own resources. When they get into scrapes, they don’t count on their fellow Harpers to rescue them. Nevertheless, Harpers are dedicated to helping one another in times of need, and friendships between Harpers are nigh unbreakable. Masterful spies and infiltrators, they use various guises and secret identities to form relationships, cultivate their information networks, and manipulate others into doing what needs to be done. Although most Harpers prefer to operate in the shadows, there are exceptions. - For this faction's Member Benefits, you can either roll for or choose a Harper Trinket. - For the Renown Score 3+ benefit, you don't need to expend 1 GP per DTP expended while outside ----- if you are at a location with an accessible Harper stronghold. - For the Renown Score 5+ benefit, you can purchase T0 potions or scrolls at their listed base cost in Allowed Content (Loot) at Harper strongholds. - The Renown Score 50+ benefit can be reused the next time your Renown score increases by 10 or more since you last used it.
Harper Token (HB)		----- Homebrew	Wondrous Item	T0	Wondrous Item, T0  This silver brooch is embossed with the symbol of the Harpers, depicting a harp against a crescent moon. This brooch is presented to newly inducted members of the Harpers to serve as faction insignia as a token of their membership.  While wearing this brooch, you gain the following benefit.  Telepathic Detection. The pin grows warm if a creature within 30 feet of yourself attempts to use telepathy to communicate.				- This homebrew item was created by greensprout - This item can be awarded using an item slot as normal but can only be obtained by a PC with a Renown Score of 3+ with the Harpers. It can't be sold or traded and the PC can't use it if they ever fall below the required Renown Score threshold. - A DM with a level 1+ DMPC with a Renown Score of 3+ with the Harpers can choose to obtain this item in replacement of their normal DM Loot for that DMPC. - NOTE: This is not a Renown Perk and is not obtained just from gaining Renown Score, but instead as an item reward.

You are immune to magic that allows other creatures to read your thoughts, determine whether you are lying, know your alignment, or know your creature type. Creatures can telepathically communicate with you only if you allow it.  Telepathic Detection. The pin grows warm if a creature within 30 feet of yourself attempts to use telepathy to communicate.				- This homebrew item was created by greensprout - This item can be awarded using an item slot as normal but can only be obtained by a PC with a Renown Score of 10+ with the Harpers. It can't be sold or traded and the PC can't use it if they ever fall below the required Renown Score threshold. - A DM with a level 1+ DMPC with a Renown Score of 10+ with the Harpers can choose to obtain this item in replacement of their normal DM Loot for that DMPC. - This homebrew item was previously known as Harper Pin - NOTE: This is not a Renown Perk and is not obtained just from gaining Renown Score, but instead as an item reward. 
Harpspeak		----- Homebrew	Story Outcome	T0	Story Outcome, T0  You've proven yourself to be a reliable Harper agent. You learn Harpspeak, a secret non-verbal language and means of communication known only to Harpers. Harpspeak can be used either in the form of a sign language or with scribed runes to leave hidden messages.   As a sign language, Harpspeak uses ordinary gestures in patterns that allow for messages to be hidden in seemingly normal and innocuous gestures such as smiles, frowns, and winks. Only a creature that knows Harpspeak understands such messages. It takes four times longer to convey such a message than it does to speak the same idea plainly.   As scribed runes, Harpspeak can be used to leave behind hidden messages for other Harpers. Creatures who know Harpspeak automatically spot such messages. Others spot the message's presence with a successful DC 15 Wisdom (Perception) check but can't decipher it without magic. Harpers sometimes mark the interior of these runes with extra dots to leave behind a misleading message for their enemies and to alert their fellow Harpers that the true symbol and its meaning is somewhere else nearby.				- This Story Outcome was created by greensprout - This Story Outcome can be awarded without using an item slot as normal but can only be obtained by a PC with a Renown Score of 15+ with the Harpers. It can't be sold or traded and the PC can't use it if they ever fall below the required Renown Score threshold. - A DM with a level 1+ DMPC with a Renown Score of 15+ with the Harpers can choose to obtain this Story Outcome in replacement of their normal DM Loot for that DMPC. - NOTE: This is not a Renown Perk and is not obtained just from gaining Renown Score, but instead as a Story Outcome.
Chord of Calling		----- Homebrew	Story Outcome	T0	Story Outcome, T0  You are a trusted voice among the Harpers and a pillar of communities, recognized finding and rooting out injustice where you can. During adventures in Faerûn, you can spend 7 days to call upon major aid from the Harpers and obtain assistance from Harper spellcasters or other support. The nature of this assistance is determined by the DM.				- This Story Outcome was created by greensprout - This Story Outcome can be awarded without using an item slot as normal but can only be obtained by a PC with a Renown Score of 25+ with the Harpers. It can't be sold or traded and the PC can't use it if they ever fall below the required Renown Score threshold. - A DM with a level 1+ DMPC with a Renown Score of 25+ with the Harpers can choose to obtain this Story Outcome in replacement of their normal DM Loot for that DMPC. - This Story Outcome was previously known as Wise Owl's Wiles - NOTE: This is not a Renown Perk and is not obtained just from gaining Renown Score, but instead as a Story Outcome.
Greater Harper Pin (HB)		----- Homebrew	Wondrous Item	T4 Select	Wondrous Item, T4 (Requires Attunement)  This silver brooch is embossed with the symbol of the Harpers, depicting a harp against a crescent moon. This brooch is presented to those that have become a High Harper, the highest rank in the leadership of the Harpers. Unlike the Harper pin provided earlier in a Harper's career, the silver of this pin has been magically treated to be as hard as adamantine and has extraordinary properties.  While wearing this pin, you gain the following effects..  Mind Shielding. You have Advantage on saving throws made to avoid or end the Charmed or Frightened condition. Additionally, you are immune to magic that allows other creatures to read your thoughts, determine whether you are lying, know your alignment, or know your creature type. Creatures can telepathically communicate with you only if you allow it.  Nondetection. The pin has the properties of an Amulet of Proof Against Detection and Location and the pin itself is also protected against such effects.  Resilience. You have Resistance to Lightning damage and you have Immunity to damage from the Magic Missile spell.  Telepathic Detection. The pin grows warm if a creature within 30 feet of yourself attempts to use telepathy to communicate.				- This homebrew item was created by greensprout - This item can be awarded using an item slot as normal but can only be obtained by a PC with a Renown Score of 50+ with the Harpers. It can't be sold or traded and the PC can't use it if they ever fall below the required Renown Score threshold. - A DM with a level 17+ DMPC with a Renown Score of 50+ with the Harpers can choose to obtain this item in replacement of their normal DM Loot for that DMPC. - NOTE: This is not a Renown Perk and is not obtained just from gaining Renown Score, but instead as an item reward. 
Order of the Gauntlet (Go to Renown)
Name		Source	Type	Tier	Description				Notes / Rage Advice
Order of the Gauntlet		FRHOF / FRAIF / DMG2014 / 2014 Basic Rules / Adventurers League	Group (Faction)	—	See Forgotten Realms: Heroes of Faerûn for a description, list of Renown rewards, and member benefits. See other sources for additional information.  The Order of the Gauntlet is composed of faithful and vigilant seekers of justice who protect others from the depredations of evildoers. The organization is honorable, vigilant, and zealous. Clerics, monks, and paladins of good (and often lawful good) alignments are commonly drawn to the Order of the Gauntlet.  Motto: The gods are with us. When evil strikes, we strike back.  Goals - Be armed and vigilant against evil. - Identify evil threats such as secretive power groups and inherently evil creatures. - Enforce justice. - Enact retribution against evil actions—do not strike preemptively.  Beliefs - Faith is the greatest weapon against evil—faith in one’s god, one’s friends, and one’s self. - Battling evil is an extraordinary task that requires extraordinary strength and bravery. - Punishing an evil act is just. Punishing an evil thought is not.  Ranks - Chevall: Renown Score 3+ - Marcheon: Renown Score 15+ - Whitehawk: Renown Score 25+ - Vindicator: Renown Score 40+ - Righteous Hand: Renown Score 50+  Titles - Knight of the Guard: Renown Score 5+ - Knight of the Banner: Renown Score 15+ - Knight of Justice: Renown Score 30+ - Chapter Knight: Renown Score 50+				- The Order of the Gauntlet are one of the major factions of Faerûn. - The Order of the Gauntlet are Hostile to the Cult of the Dragon, the Red Wizards, the Zhentarim, and the Legion of Dread. - Traits: The Order of the Gauntlet is a dedicated, tightly knit group of like-minded individuals driven by religious zeal or a finely-honed sense of justice and honor. Friendship and camaraderie are important to members of the order, and they share a trust and a bond normally reserved for siblings. Like highly motivated soldiers, members of the Order of the Gauntlet seek to become the best at what they do and look forward to testing their mettle. There are few, if any, “lone wolves” in this organization. - For this faction's Member Benefits, you can either roll for or choose an Order of the Gauntlet Trinket. - For the Renown Score 3+ benefit, you don't need to expend 1 GP per DTP expended while outside ----- if you are at a location with an accessible Order of the Gauntlet chapter. - For the Renown Score 10 benefit, the magic item obtained is a T1 Armor, Ring, or Weapon of your choice in Allowed Content (Loot) and must be logged in the session or downtime log in which you reached this Renown score. - For the Renown Score 15+ benefit, obtaining the services of an Order of the Gauntlet spellcaster to cast level 2 or lower Cleric spells costs no DTP or GP in downtime. However, any spells cast this way to achieve a lasting effect still cost DTP equal to the spell's level (minimum of 1 DTP). - For the Renown Score 40 benefit, the magic item obtained is a T2 Armor, Ring, or Weapon of your choice in Allowed Content (Loot) and must be logged in the session or downtime log in which you reached this Renown score.
Emblem of the Order (HB)		----- Homebrew	Wondrous Item	T0	Wondrous Item, T0  This Emblem serves as faction insignia given to new members of the Order of the Gauntlet and to mark them as belonging to the faction. Prominently displayed upon the Emblem is the symbol of the Order of the Gauntlet: a gauntleted fist grasping a sword by the blade.  You can use this Emblem as a Holy Symbol. While wearing the fabric or holding the Shield that the Emblem is borne upon, you can take a Bonus Action to cause the Emblem to shed Bright Light in a 10-foot radius and Dim Light for an additional 10 feet, or to extinguish the light.				- This homebrew item was created by greensprout - This item can be awarded using an item slot as normal but can only be obtained by a PC with a Renown Score of 3+ with the Order of the Gauntlet. It can't be sold or traded and the PC can't use it if they ever fall below the required Renown Score threshold. - A DM with a level 1+ DMPC with a Renown Score of 3+ with the Order of the Gauntlet can choose to obtain this item in replacement of their normal DM Loot for that DMPC. - NOTE: This is not a Renown Perk and is not obtained just from gaining Renown Score, but instead as an item reward.
Hero's Welcome		----- Homebrew	Story Outcome	T0	Story Outcome, T0  Word of your deeds among the Order of the Gauntlet have spread among the common folk of the Sword Coast and beyond as a champion of justice.   You can find a place to hide, rest, or recuperate among other commoners, unless you have shown yourself to be a danger to them. They will shield you from the law or anyone else searching for you and will extend you every courtesy and do their utmost to help you. Unless you have shown yourself to be a danger to them, they will even take up arms to fight alongside you, should you find yourself facing an enemy alone.				- This Story Outcome was created by greensprout - This Story Outcome can be awarded without using an item slot as normal but can only be obtained by a PC with a Renown Score of 10+ with the Order of the Gauntlet. While in Faerûn, you can expend 30 DTP and 1000 GP to purchase a Hippogriff as a downtime activity; the Hippogriff is delivered to the settlement or stronghold nearest to you where the Order of the Gauntlet is present.				- This Renown Perk was created by greensprout - This Story Outcome can be awarded without using an item slot as normal but can only be obtained by a PC with a Renown Score of 15+ with the Order of the Gauntlet. Talisman of the Gauntlet (HB)		----- Homebrew	Wondrous Item	T4 Select	Wondrous Item, T4 (Requires Attunement)  This silver talisman is emblazoned with the symbol of the Order of the Gauntlet, depicting the blade of retribution held by the gauntlet of justice. This talisman is presented to those that have become a Righteous Hand, the highest rank in the Order of the Gauntlet's leadership.   Holy Symbol. You can use the talisman as a Holy Symbol. You gain a +3 bonus to your saving throws while you wear or hold this talisman.  Holy Rebuke. This talisman has 7 charges and regains 1d6 + 1 expended charges daily at dawn. While holding or wearing this talisman, you can take a Magic action to expend 1 or more of its charges to cause one of the following effects: - Rebuke Evil. You expend 1 charge and target one creature you can see within 60 feet of yourself. The target must succeed on a DC 17 Wisdom saving throw, taking 8d6 Psychic damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one. If the target is evil, it has Disadvantage on the save.  - Turn Evil. You expend 2 charges and target one creature you can see within 60 feet of yourself, forcing the target to make a DC 17 Wisdom saving throw. On a failed save, the target has the Frightened condition for 1 minute or until it takes damage. While Frightened in this way, the target can do only one of the following on each of its turns: move or take an action or a Bonus Action. If the target is evil, it has Disadvantage on the initial save, and the target can repeat the saving throw at the end of each of its turns, ending the condition on itself on a success. - Grand Rebuke. You expend 4 charges and target each creature of your choice that you can see within 60 feet of yourself. Each target must succeed on a DC 17 Wisdom saving throw, taking 8d6 Psychic damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one. If the target is evil, it has Disadvantage on the save. 				- This homebrew item was created by greensprout - This item can be awarded using an item slot as normal but can only be obtained by a PC with a Renown Score of 50+ with the Order of the Gauntlet. It can't be sold or traded and the PC can't use it if they ever fall below the required Renown Score threshold. - A DM with a level 17+ DMPC with a Renown Score of 50+ with the Order of the Gauntlet can choose to obtain this item in replacement of their normal DM Loot for that DMPC. - NOTE: This is not a Renown Perk and is not obtained just from gaining Renown Score, but instead as an item reward. 
Emerald Enclave (Go to Renown)
Name		Source	Type	Tier	Description				Notes / Rage Advice
Emerald Enclave		FRHOF / FRAIF / DMG2014 / 2014 Basic Rules / Adventurers League	Group (Faction)	—	See Forgotten Realms: Heroes of Faerûn for a description, list of Renown rewards, and member benefits. See other sources for additional information.  The Emerald Enclave is a widespread group of wilderness survivalists who preserve the natural order while rooting out unnatural threats. The organization is decentralized, hardy, and reclusive. Barbarians, druids, and rangers of good or neutral alignments are commonly drawn to the Emerald Enclave.  Motto: We preserve the natural order.  Goals - Restore and preserve the natural order. - Destroy all that is unnatural. - Keep the elemental forces of the world in check. - Keep civilization and the wilderness from destroying each other.  Beliefs - The natural order must be respected and preserved. - Forces that upset the natural order must be destroyed. - Civilization and the wilderness must learn to coexist peacefully.  Ranks - Springwarden: Renown Score 3+ - Summerstrider: Renown Score 15+ - Autumnreaver: Renown Score 25+ - Winterstalker: Renown Score 40+ - Master of the Wild: Renown Score 50+				- The Emerald Enclave are one of the major factions of Faerûn. - The Emerald Enclave are Hostile to the Cult of the Dragon, the Red Wizards, the Zhentarim, and the Legion of Dread. - Traits: Members of the Emerald Enclave are spread far and wide, and usually operate in isolation. They learn to depend on themselves more than others. Survival in a harsh world also demands great fortitude and mastery of certain fighting and survival skills. Members of the Enclave who dedicate themselves to helping others survive the perils of the wilderness are more social than others who are charged with defending sacred glades and preserving the natural balance. - For this faction's Member Benefits, you can either roll for or choose an Emerald Enclave Trinket. Additionally, you don't need to expend 1 GP per DTP expended while outside ----- and while in Faerûn. - The Renown Score 5+ benefit can only be used during adventures or as overseen by a DM. - For the Renown Score 15+ benefit, you can purchase T0 or T1 potions or spell scrolls at their listed base cost in Allowed Content (Loot) at locations where the Emerald Enclave is present. - For the Renown Score 30 benefit, the number of Potions of Healing (Superior) obtained must be rolled and logged in the session or downtime log in which you reached this Renown score. - The Renown Score 50+ benefit can be reused the next time your Renown score increases by 5 or more since you last used it.

Wildmark (HB)		----- Homebrew	Supernatural Gift	T4 Select	Supernatural Gift, T4  As a Master of the Wild of the Emerald Enclave, you've achieved the highest rank of leadership within the faction and have been inducted into its deepest mysteries. While this Supernatural Gift is active, you gain the following traits.  Nature's Protection. You are immune to curses, disease, and magical contagions and have the benefits of an Amulet of Proof Against Detection and Location. Additionally, you stop aging: you are immune to any effect that would age you, and you can't die from old age.  Nature's Blessing. When you select this Supernatural Gift to be active when finishing a Long Rest, choose one of the following options below to benefit from. You can only benefit from one option at a time. - Blessing of Eldath. You can cast the Water Breathing spell at will, requiring no components. Additionally, you can cast the Plane Shift spell, requiring no components and targeting yourself only, with the destination being a location on the Elemental Plane of Water. After you cast Plane Shift in this way, you can't do so again until you finish a Long Rest. - Blessing of Mielikki. You can take a Magic action to shape-shift into a Treant. You retain your game statistics and ability to speak, but your creature type, size, AC, movement modes, Strength, and Dexterity are replaced by those of the Treant stat block, and you gain any statistics and capabilities that the Treant stat block has that you lack. You remain shape-shifted in this way until you are reduced to 0 Hit Points, die, or end it as a Bonus Action. After you shape-shift in this way, you can't do so again until you finish a Short or Long Rest. - Blessing of Silvanus. You can cast the Meld into Stone spell at will, requiring no components. Additionally, you can cast the Plane Shift spell, requiring no components and targeting yourself only, with the destination being a location on the Elemental Plane of Earth. After you cast Plane Shift in this way, you can't do so again until you finish a Long Rest.				- This homebrew Supernatural Gift was created by greensprout - This Supernatural Gift can be awarded using an item slot as normal but can only be obtained by a PC with a Renown Score of 50+ with the Emerald Enclave. Lords' Alliance (Go to Renown)
Name		Source	Type	Tier	Description				Notes / Rage Advice
Lords' Alliance		FRHOF / FRAIF / DMG2014 / 2014 Basic Rules / Adventurers League	Group (Faction)	—	See Forgotten Realms: Heroes of Faerûn for a description, list of Renown rewards, and member benefits. See other sources for additional information.  The Lords’ Alliance is a loose coalition of established political powers concerned with mutual security and prosperity. The organization is aggressive, militant, and political. Fighters and sorcerers of lawful or neutral alignments are commonly drawn to the Lords’ Alliance.  Motto: Threats to home must be terminated without prejudice. Superiority is our security.   Goals - Ensure the safety and prosperity of cities and other settlements of Faerûn. - Maintain a strong coalition against the forces of disorder. - Proactively eliminate threats to the established powers. - Bring honor and glory to one’s leaders and one’s homeland.  Beliefs - If civilization is to survive, all must unite against the dark forces that threaten it. - Fight for your realm. Only you can bring honor, glory, and prosperity to your lord and homeland. - Don’t wait for the enemy to come to you. The best defense is a strong offense.  Ranks - Cloak: Renown Score 3+ - Redknife: Renown Score 15+ - Stingblade: Renown Score 25+ - Warduke: Renown Score 40+ - Lioncrown: Renown Score 50+				- The Lords' Alliance are one of the major factions of Faerûn. - The Lords' Alliance is Hostile to the Cult of the Dragon, the Red Wizards, the Zhentarim, and the Legion of Dread. - Traits: To seek out and destroy threats to their homelands, agents of the Lords’ Alliance must be highly trained at what they do. Few can match their skills in the field. They fight for the glory and the security of their people and for the lords who rule over them, and they do so with pride. However, the Lords’ Alliance can only survive if its members “play nice” with one another, which requires a certain measure of diplomacy. Rogue agents within the Lords’ Alliance are rare, but defections have been known to occur. - For this faction's Member Benefits, you can either roll for or choose a Lords' Alliance Trinket. Additionally, you don't need to expend 1 GP per DTP expended while outside ----- and while in a Lords' Alliance settlement.  - For the Renown Score 5+ benefit, level 0 - 3 spell scrolls acquired at a Lords' Alliance settlement in this way can be purchased for their listed base cost value in Allowed Content (Loot), to which the listed 10% discount is applied. - For the Renown Score 10 benefit, the magic item obtained is a T1 magic item of your choice in Allowed Content (Loot) and must be logged in the session or downtime log in which you reached this Renown score. - For the Renown Score 25+ benefit, obtaining the services of a Lords' Alliance spellcaster in a Lords' Alliance settlement to cast the Sending spell costs no DTP or GP in downtime.

While wearing this crown, you can expend 1 or more of the crown's charges to cast one of the following spells from it: Aid (level 4 version, 4 charges), Circle of Power (5 charges), Crusader's Mantle (3 charges), or Heroism (5 Temporary Hit Points, 1 charge).				- This homebrew item was created by greensprout - This homebrew item can be awarded using an item slot as normal but can only be obtained by a PC with a Renown Score of 50+ with the Lords' Alliance. Zhentarim (Go to Renown)
Name		Source	Type	Tier	Description				Notes / Rage Advice
Zhentarim		FRHOF / FRAIF / DMG2014 / 2014 Basic Rules / Adventurers League	Group (Faction)	—	See Forgotten Realms: Heroes of Faerûn for a description, list of Renown rewards, and member benefits. See other sources for additional information.  The Zhentarim is an unscrupulous shadow network that seeks to expand its influence and power throughout Faerûn. The organization is ambitious, opportunistic, and meritocratic. Rogues and warlocks of neutral and/or evil alignments are commonly drawn to the Zhentarim.  Motto: Join us and prosper. Oppose us and suffer.  Goals - Amass wealth. - Look for opportunities to seize power. - Gain influence over important people and organizations. - Dominate Faerûn.  Beliefs - The Zhentarim is your family. You watch out for it, and it watches out for you. - You are the master of your own destiny. Never be less than what you deserve to be. - Everything—and everyone—has a price.• The Zhentarim is your family. You watch out for it, and it watches out for you. - You are the master of your own destiny. Never be less than what you deserve to be. - Everything—and everyone—has a price.  Ranks - Fang: Renown Score 3+ - Wolf: Renown Score 10+ - Viper: Renown Score 25+ - Ardragon: Renown Score 40+ - Dread Lord: Renown Score 50+				- The Zhentarim are one of the major factions of Faerûn. - The Zhentarim is Hostile to the Emerald Enclave, the Harpers, the Lords' Alliance, the Order of the Gauntlet, and the Legion of Dread. - Traits: A member of the Zhentarim thinks of himself or herself as a member of a large family, and relies on the Black Network for resources and security. However, members are granted enough autonomy to pursue their own interests and gain some measure of personal power or influence. The Black Network is a meritocracy. It promises “the best of the best,” although in truth, the Zhentarim is more interested in spreading its own propaganda and influence than investing in the improvement of its individual members. - For this faction's Member Benefits, you can either roll for or choose a Zhentarim Trinket. - For the Renown Score 3+ benefit, you don't need to expend 1 GP per DTP expended while outside ----- if you are at a Zhentarim-controlled safe house or inn. - For the Renown Score 10 benefit, the magic item obtained is a T1 magic item of your choice in Allowed Content (Loot) and must be logged in the session or downtime log in which you reached this Renown score. - For the Renown Score 40 benefit, you can't call on the aid of a Zhentarim-allied Beholder for help again until your Renown Score increases by 10 or more since the last time you called upon one. - For the Renown Score 50 benefit, gaining the benefit of the Clone spell in this way costs no GP or DTP. If the Clone was used, you can't call on the aid of a Zhentarim spellcaster to cast the Clone spell in this way for you again until your Renown Score increases by 10 or more since you last used a Clone created by this benefit.
Zhentarim Token (HB)		----- Homebrew	Trinket	T0	Trinket, T0  This iron coin is stamped with the symbol of the Zhentarim, a winged snake biting a coin, and is given to newly inducted members of the Black Network and serves as faction insignia to mark membership within the faction.   When you display this coin, you have Advantage on Intimidation checks made against merchants, travelers, and others that fear and respect the influence of the Black Network.				- This homebrew item was created by greensprout - This item can be awarded using an item slot as normal but can only be obtained by a PC with a Renown Score of 3+ with the Zhentarim. It can't be sold or traded and the PC can't use it if they ever fall below the required Renown Score threshold. - A DM with a level 1+ DMPC with a Renown Score of 3+ with the Zhentarim can choose to obtain this item in replacement of their normal DM Loot for that DMPC. - NOTE: This is not a Renown Perk and is not obtained just from gaining Renown Score, but instead as an item reward. 
Black Network Connections		----- Homebrew	Story Outcome	T0	Story Outcome, T0  You are a champion of the Zhentarim, with your actions recognized to have brought significant benefit and respect to the Black Network. During adventures in Faerûn, you can spend 7 days to call upon major aid from the Zhentarim and obtain assistance from Zhentarim spellcasters or other support. The nature of this assistance is determined by the DM.				- This Renown Perk was created by greensprout - This Story Outcome can be awarded without using an item slot as normal but can only be obtained by a PC with a Renown Score of 25+ with the Zhentarim. It can't be sold or traded and the PC can't use it if they ever fall below the required Renown Score threshold. - A DM with a level 1+ DMPC with a Renown Score of 25+ with the Zhentarim can choose to obtain this Story Outcome in replacement of their normal DM Loot for that DMPC. - This Story Outcome was previously known as Ardragon's Authority - NOTE: This is not a Renown Perk and is not obtained just from gaining Renown Score, but instead as a Story Outcome. 
Zhentilar Tattoo (HB)		----- Homebrew	Wondrous Item (Tattoo)	T2 Select	Wondrous Item (Tattoo), T2  Produced by a special needle, this magic tattoo appears on your body as a Flying Snake which moves about your skin to avoid being seen by others. This tattoo is given to committed members of the Zhentarim, marking their irrevocable dedication to the Black Network for the remainder of their life.  Zhentilar Dedication. To apply this tattoo, you hold the needle to your skin where you want the tattoo to appear, pressing the needle there for duration of a Short Rest. When you finish the Short Rest, the needle turns into the ink that has become the tattoo, which appears on the skin. After the tattoo is applied, it cannot be removed from you unless you die or are targeted with a Wish spell or Divine Intervention, in which case the tattoo vanishes and the needle reappears in your space.   Tattoo Attunement. You can only attune to this tattoo if it has been applied to you.  Shadowy Acts (Requires Attunement). You have Advantage on Dexterity (Stealth) and Charisma (Intimidation) checks. Additionally, while you are in Dim Light or Darkness, you can Hide as a Bonus Action even if you are being directly observed.  Zhentarim Diplomacy (Requires Attunement). This tattoo has 6 charges and regains 1d6 expended charges daily at dusk. You can expend 1 or more of the tattoo's charges to cast one of the following spells from it (save DC 15): Command (1 charge), Fear (3 charges), or Suggestion (2 charges).				- This homebrew item was created by greensprout - This item can be awarded using an item slot as normal but can only be obtained by a PC with a Renown Score of 30+ with the Zhentarim. It can't be sold or traded and the PC can't use it if they ever fall below the required Renown Score threshold. - A DM with a level 5+ DMPC with a Renown Score of 30+ with the Zhentarim can choose to obtain this item in replacement of their normal DM Loot for that DMPC. - NOTE: This is not a Renown Perk and is not obtained just from gaining Renown Score, but instead as an item reward. 
Faceless Eyes of the Zhentarim		----- Homebrew	Wondrous Item	T4 Select	Wondrous Item, T4 (Requires Attunement)  This stylized hood of black and gold leaves only the eyes visible. This hood is immediately recognizable as a symbol of the Black Network and is often presented to those that have become a Dread Lord, the highest rank in the Zhentarim's leadership.  Faceless Gaze. To gaze upon the faceless eyes of this hood is to invite fear. When a creature starts its turn within 30 feet of you and can see you, you can force it to make a DC 19 Wisdom saving throw if you don't have the Incapacitated condition and can see the creature. On a failed save, the creature has the Frightened condition until the start of its next turn, and if the saving throw fails by 5 or more, the creature's Speed is reduced to 0 while Frightened in this way. Unless surprised, a creature can avert its eyes to avoid the saving throw at the start of its next turn. If the creature does so, it can't see you until the start of its next turn, when it can avert its eyes again. If the creature looks at you in the meantime, it must immediately make the save.  Mark for Death. You can take a Magic action to choose one creature you can see within 30 feet of you, marking it until the end of your next turn. The next time you deal damage to the marked creature, the creature has Vulnerability to all of that source's damage, and then the mark ends.				- This homebrew item was created by greensprout - This homebrew item can be awarded using an item slot as normal but can only be obtained by a PC with a Renown Score of 50+ with the Zhentarim. It can't be sold or traded and the PC can't use it if they ever fall below the required Renown Score threshold. - A DM with a level 17+ DMPC with a Renown Score of 50+ with the Zhentarim can choose to obtain this homebrew item in replacement of their normal DM Loot for that DMPC. - NOTE: This is not a Renown Perk and is not obtained just from gaining Renown Score, but instead as an item reward.

Story Outcomes
Name		Source	Type	Tier	Description				Notes / Rage Advice
Story Outcome		Homebrew by a DM	Story Outcome	T0	A narrative consequence gained as a result of events that occur as part of an adventure, as determined and designed by a DM.				- A Story Outcome is either a positive or negative narrative consequence as a result of events during an adventure. For example, as a result of an adventure, friendly NPCs in a region may provide shelter to that character in the future if they adventure in the same region again. - A Story Outcome only comes into play in future games at the discretion of the presiding DM. - The mechanical effect of a Story Outcome if any shouldn't exceed the gold and loot allotment of the adventure or have a mechanical effect greater than that of a minor magical property (excluding Guardian or Unbreakable). - Awarding more mechanically significant Story Outcomes requires a Rules request in #dm-requests.

# Item Properties

Jump to
Magic Item Special Features				Other
Creator	History	Minor Property	Quirk	Sentient Magic Items
Any Item Properties listed as Fair Game are available to be awarded by any DM. Any Item Properties listed as Full DM Only can only be awarded by Full DMs (or via #dm-collaboration with a Full DM)
Name		Source	Type	Category	Description					Notes / Rage Advice
Magic Item Special Features
A magic item can have Special Features as described in the 2014 or 2024 Dungeon Master's Guide for loot awarded by a DM as logged in #treasure-log and #session-log
A magic item can have up to 1 Creator, up to 1 History, up to 2 Minor Properties, and up to 1 Quirk in this way
If a Special Feature grants a numerical bonus, you can benefit from only one bonus of the same kind that adds to the same value. For example, if you have two magic items with the Guardian minor property, you only benefit from one of them, not both.

Minor Property
Name		Source	Type	Category	Description					Notes / Rage Advice
Custom Minor Property		Homebrew by a Full DM	Minor Property	Full DM Only	A custom item property as created by a Full DM that details a minor property the magic item has.					- A Full DM can create and award a Minor Property not listed here (or via #dm-collaboration with a Full DM) - Any custom Minor Property created in this way must be a minor property: DMs are advised to refer to the Minor Properties listed here for the scope of mechanical effect appropriate for a Minor Property. - As a general rule of thumb, a Minor Property provides an additional minor effect that doesn't directly interact with or affect how the item is meant to be used nor does it workaround how it is meant to be used. For example, a property that increased the speed conferred by a set of Wayfarer's Boots would not be a Minor Property. Likewise, a property that allowed you to retain the magical bonus of a +1 Shield without wielding it also wouldn't be a Minor Property. In both examples, the properties directly affect the normal use and properties of the item. - As a general rule of thumb, a Minor Property either consists of a cosmetic effect or has a mechanical benefit that is often conditional or niche in applicability and with limited overall effect. - As a general rule of thumb, if a Minor Property would replicate an effect only seen in a T1 or higher magic item or a magic item that requires Attunement, then it is not appropriate to be a Minor Property. - Any property that directly improves attack rolls, saving throws, AC, or HP or likewise impose a similar penalty on enemies is not appropriate as a Minor Property. - If a Full DM that is ever uncertain if a property they designed for an upcoming game is truly appropriate to be a Minor Property, they can consult with other DMs for advice in #dm-collaboration or contact a member of the Rules team for assistance.

Other
Sentient Magic Items
Any magic item can be a sentient magic item using the rules in the 2014 or 2024 Dungeon Master's Guide for loot awarded by a DM as logged in #treasure-log and #session-logs 
Only Full DMs (or DMs collaborating with one via #dm-collaboration) can award sentient magic items in this way.
Existing examples of sentient magic items present in the 2014 or 2024 Dungeon Master's Guide can be referenced for how to format the Sentience and Personality properties of a sentient magic item.
Name		Source	Type	Category	Description					Notes / Rage Advice
Abilities		DMG2014/2024	Sentient Magic Item Property	Full DM Only	The sentient magic item's Intelligence, Wisdom, and Charisma ability scores. See sources for description.					As described, either choose the sentient magic item's Intelligence, Wisdom, and Charisma scores, or determine them by rolling 4d6, dropping the lowest and totaling the rest.
Alignment		DMG2014/2024	Sentient Magic Item Property	Full DM Only	The sentient magic item's alignment. See sources for description.					As described, either choose the sentient magic item's alignment or roll on the associated table to determine it.
Communication		DMG2014/2024	Sentient Magic Item Property	Full DM Only	The sentient magic item's means of communication. See sources for description.					- As described, either choose the sentient magic item's means of communication on the associated table or roll on the associated table to determine it. - You can also have the sentient magic item communicate in a unique way not listed in the table, such as having words appear on the item.
Senses		DMG2014/2024	Sentient Magic Item Property	Full DM Only	The sentient magic item's senses. See sources for description.					As described, either choose the sentient magic item's senses on the associated table or roll on the associated table to determine it.
Special Purpose		DMG2014/2024	Sentient Magic Item Property	Full DM Only	The sentient magic item's special purpose. See sources for description.					- As described, either choose the sentient magic item's special purpose on the associated table or roll on the associated table to determine it. - You can also assign a unique special purpose to the sentient magic item not listed on the table, such as seeking to reclaim a lost throne.
Characteristics		DMG2014	Sentient Magic Item Property	Full DM Only	The sentient magic item's characteristics and personality. See source for description.					As described, determine the sentient magic item's characteristics and personality by using the guidelines for creating NPCs, including determing personality traits, ideals, bonds, and flaws.

# Monsters

Jump to
Fair Game					Full DM Only
Core	Supplemental	Adventures	Non-FR		Core	Supplemental	Adventures	Non-FR
Except with a DM's permission during an adventure, only monsters in Fair Game as printed can be used by PCs for purposes of spells and game features that let them summon or shape-shift into a creature.  Statblocks of unique NPCs and monster variants, even if reflavored, cannot be used in this way except with a DM's permission during an adventure.
DMs should take care to review the statblock, lore, and Notes / Rage Advice for any monster they plan to include in a game.
Name		Source		Category	CR	Creature Type		Notes / Rage Advice
Fair Game
Fair Game Monsters can be used by any DM in adventures they run.

Flail Snail		Volo's Guide to Monsters		Fair Game	3.0	Elemental		- An intact shell to be sold for gold counts against the adventure's gold allotment and can only be recovered intact if it fits the gold allotment for the adventure. - An intact shell to be recovered to make a Robe of Scintillating Colors counts against the adventure's item slots as a T3 permanent item and can only be recovered for this purpose if it fits the item slots and required APL for the adventure. - An intact shell to be recovered to make up to 3 shields with the Antimagic Shell trait that become Spellguard Shields counts against the adventure's item slots as a T3 item each and can only be recovered for this purpose if it fits the item slots and required APL for the adventure. The shield retains the Antimagic Shell trait for 30 days, with each in character day that passes during an adventure counting towards the time after which the shield becomes a Spellguard Shield. Each real life day that passes while the character is not adventuring as part of a multisession or campaign does not count towards this duration.

Flail Snail		Mordenkainen Presents: Monsters of the Multiverse		Fair Game	3.0	Elemental		- An intact shell to be sold for gold counts against the adventure's gold allotment and can only be recovered intact if it fits the gold allotment for the adventure. - An intact shell to be recovered to make a Robe of Scintillating Colors counts against the adventure's item slots as a T3 permanent item and can only be recovered for this purpose if it fits the item slots and required APL for the adventure. - An intact shell to be recovered to make up to 3 shields with the Antimagic Shell trait that become Spellguard Shields counts against the adventure's item slots as a T3 item each and can only be recovered for this purpose if it fits the item slots and required APL for the adventure. The shield retains the Antimagic Shell trait for 30 days, with each in character day that passes during an adventure counting towards the time after which the shield becomes a Spellguard Shield. Each real life day that passes while the character is not adventuring as part of a multisession or campaign does not count towards this duration.

Loup Garou		Van Richten's Guide to Ravenloft		Fair Game	13.0	Monstrosity (Shapechanger)		- As per server rules, a PC cursed with lycanthropy normally can't be played in another game until the lycanthropy is removed and becomes an NPC under the DM's control during the full moon. Given the nature of loup garou lycanthropy, the character can be only be played in adventures taken for the purpose of finding and killing the loup garou that cursed them so as to make it possible to cure their lycanthropy. - Lycanthropy from this creature can also be removed via a Wish spell or Divine Intervention (2014) even if the loup garou is still alive. A PC can benefit from Divine Intervention (2014) to cure loup garou lycanthropy in this way only once in their career. - After the loup garou has been slain and if Remove Curse is used on the cursed PC, the affected PC must roll their DC 17 Constitution saving throw in #downtime-logs. On a successful save, the PC must then expend 3 DTP to clear exhaustion accrued from the ordeal. On a failed save, the PC must either wait 30 real life days or expend 30 DTP (or the remainder in DTP of days remaining) before another attempt at Remove Curse can be made.

Full DM Only
Full DM Only monsters can only be used by Full DMs in adventures they run, regardless of if they show up in a non-combat or combat capacity. Any major named NPC is considered to be a Full DM Only monster.
The use of Full DM Only monsters in combat should avoid sweeping changes, especially those that would prevent their use by other DMs, without collaborating with other DMs and Lore.
